# 序

习近平主席在致2024世界中文大会的贺信中指出，语言是人类交流思想的工具、传承文明的载体、增进理解的桥梁。中文承载着中华民族数千年的文明智慧，是中国贡献给世界的重要公共文化产品，支持服务国际社会开展好中文教育是中国作为母语国的责任。

作为中国教育部推出的服务全球中文学习者的标准化语言测量工具，中文水平考试HSK历经三十余年发展，已成为全球最具影响力和权威性的中文水平测评体系。截至目前，HSK已在全球168个国家设立1477个考点、3万余间考场，累计服务考生820万人。HSK不仅成为评估国际中文教育质量的重要标尺，更在吸引国际学生来华留学、助力海外人才在中资及跨国企业就业实习等方面发挥着日益重要的作用。

当前，新一轮科技革命与产业变革加速演进，人工智能等新技术正在重塑教育形态，对人才需求产生深远影响。面对海外中文学习需求的爆发式增长，国际中文教育迎来新的发展机遇，也对HSK的服务能力与测评体系提出更高要求。为此，我们积极推进HSK向3.0版本升级，将原有一至六级考试体系调整为覆盖初、中、高级各水平人群的三等九级新体系，并根据社会经济生活发展，调整HSK话题和词汇，增加中国文化、中国当代发展等考查内容，帮助全球学习者了解真实、立体、全面的中国；加强“中文+学术”“中文+专业”“中文+职业”等内容，帮助国际人才精准掌握中文、拓宽专业领域；推动人工智能全面赋能测评体系，加强AI命题、AI评分及AI自适应学习产品开发，主动适应并引领智能时代的教育变革。

以HSK3.0为依托，汉考国际（CTI）联合外语教学与研究出版社，汇聚专家智慧，开发了《新HSK教程》。本教程坚持以学习者为中心，支持课堂教学、自主学习和AI助学等多种方式，旨在全面提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养。很高兴能向各国中文学习者推荐《新HSK教程》，期待这套教程能够助力更多国际学子掌握中文、走向世界，为推动构建人类命运共同体贡献语言之力！

最后，谨向所有参与HSK发展与教程编写的专家同仁、全球合作伙伴致以诚挚谢意！

郁云峰

中国教育部中外语言交流合作中心主任

2025年12月

#### 前言

作为新一代多功能教材，《新HSK教程》系列（以下简称“新教程”）的设计、编写继续秉持HSK“以学习者为中心”的理念，并综合借鉴当代语言学、第二语言教学等理论成果，进一步拓展实践空间。“以学习者为中心”的理念强调新教程不是单一的HSK考试用书，而是将HSK考试与中文教学、学习有机融合，通过课堂教学、自主学习、AI助学的方式进一步提升学习者的中文交际能力、认知水平与思辨素养，帮助其成为精通中文、融通中外的优秀人才。

新教程1～6级分为主干课本和学练手册两种，主干课本聚焦课堂教学与核心内容传授，学练手册则是对主干课本内容的延伸和补充。学练手册练习题深度对标HSK3.0考试，学习者在练习的过程中既能了解自身中文掌握情况，也能熟悉HSK3.0考试题型，从而实现从容备考。此外，新教程的主干课本在编写中突出交际情景化、结构简约化，同时借助数字手段使教材资源呈现立体化特征。

由于中文对交际语境高度依赖，情景的重要性不言而喻。为此，新教程借鉴情景剧形式设计交际场景及交际任务，以一系列独立的情景剧单元组织内容。例如，新教程设计了海外和中国国内两类场景，故事线索围绕数位主要角色展开，不仅演绎中文学习者的校园生活、个人成长点滴，也展现中国人的日常生活、学习工作图景，讲述中外交往、友谊生辉的故事，让学习者在真实的场景和故事中学习中文，了解真实、立体、全面的中国。

为满足AI时代的泛在化学习需求，新教程采取教案化、学案化设计流程，更方便教学或自学。新教程编写期间，恰逢AI技术蓬勃发展，数字技术的全方位赋能使得教材资源立体化建设成为可能。新教程设计了一位AI助教——小语，她是将纸版教材和数字资源连接起来的灵魂性人物，学习者找到小语，即可获得相关资源。例如，学习者可以通过“小语助力”“小语讲堂”等板块进行语言知识及语言技能的学习，通过敲“小语的彩蛋”获取更多数字资源，既有趣又方便。未来我们还将持续开发线上课程，让小语成为您教中文、学中文的好助手。

新教程策划及编写过程中，得到了中外语言交流合作中心、汉考国际、外语教学与研究出版社以及业内许多专家学者的鼎力支持与专业指导。现在，这套凝聚着集体智慧的教材终于和大家见面了，我们诚挚欢迎海内外使用者及时反馈使用中遇到的问题，帮助新教程不断完善。感谢您的支持！

编者

2025年12月

# 使用说明

《新HSK教程2》适用于已学习30～36学时、掌握约300个汉语词汇及预备参加HSK（二级）考试的学习者。

##### 基本设计

依据HSK新大纲和本系列教程设计理念、原则，《新HSK教程2》全书共设15课，词汇匹配新大纲规定的200词，略有扩展，语言点45个。总授课时数建议30～45学时，每课建议授课时间为2～3学时。

《新HSK教程2》继续秉持“以学习者为中心”理念，坚持目标导向、需求导向，以培养学习者中文交际能力为原则，课文内容、语言点等与新HSK任务、话题、词汇、语法及汉字大纲一致。实际教学、学习中，建议按照教材结构顺序有序推进。

##### 课文板块

本书课文板块包括目标、热身、课文、小语讲堂、综合练习、课堂活动、小语的彩蛋、学习小结等。

目标：提示当课教学、学习重点。

热身：由两个板块构成，板块一是词图匹配，用于检查学习者预习词语的情况；板块二包括词图匹配、填空、完成表格等，用于语言点的导入或练习。

课文：每课包含四篇课文，前三篇为对话体，是情景对话；第四篇为叙述体。

小语讲堂：为语言点讲练。随文出现，例句后有练习，随学随练。

综合练习：为当课词语和语言点的综合性练习。教师可灵活安排，或课上完成，或布置为课后作业。

课堂活动：设置角色扮演、双人活动、小组调查等，综合运用所学内容，完成交际任务。

小语的彩蛋：本书共设置九个。主要为与当课相关的必要的信息及文化点等，是课文内容的延伸与补充。

学习小结：学习后的总结与反思。每三课设置一个，供师生评估和反思教学、学习效果。

此外，本书还设置了“小语助力”，一般直接置于相关内容附近，提示、注解重要信息，帮助学习者简单快速了解相关知识。

本书还配有学练手册及数字教材。数字教材可通过“外研国际智慧教育平台”（https://global.unipus.cn）获取。数字教材涵盖教材配套的音频、视频、交互式学习内容、补充练习等丰富资源。数字教材激活说明请扫描封底二维码查看。本书配套资源可通过“外研社汉语教学资源网”（http://www.fltrp-clt.com）查看并下载。

# Instructions for Use

New HSK Course 2 is designed for learners who have completed approximately 30–36 class hours, mastered around 300 vocabulary words, and are preparing to take the HSK (Level 2) exam.

##### Basic Design

Based on the new HSK syllabus and the design philosophy and principles of this textbook series, New HSK Course 2 consists of 15 lessons, includes vocabulary aligned with the 200 words required by the new syllabus with slight extensions, and covers 45 language points. The recommended total teaching time is 30–45 class hours, with 2–3 class hours suggested for each lesson.

New HSK Course 2 continues to follow a learner-centered philosophy and adopts a goal-oriented needs-based approach, with the principle of cultivating learners' Chinese communicative competence. The content and language points of the lessons are consistent with the new HSK syllabus, including tasks, topics, vocabulary, grammar, and characters. In actual teaching and learning, it is recommended to progress in an orderly manner according to the structure of the textbook.

##### Lesson Sections

The lesson sections of this book include Objectives, Warm-Ups, Texts, Xiaoyu's Classroom, Comprehensive Exercises, Classroom Activity, Xiaoyu's Bonus Content, and Learning Summary.

Objectives: Key points of teaching and learning in each lesson.

Warm-Up: A two-part activity that includes a word-picture matching section to assess learners' preparation of vocabulary, and a second section featuring word-picture matching, fill-in-the-blank, and table completion for introducing or practicing language points.

Texts: Four texts, with the first three being situational dialogues and the fourth being a narrative text.

Xiaoyu's Classroom: Explanations and practice of language points, appearing after each text with exercises following example sentences for practice.

Comprehensive Exercises: Integrated exercises for vocabulary and language points of the current lesson, arranged flexibly for in-class or after-class use.

Classroom Activity: Role-play, pair work, and group survey are designed to help students integrate what they have learned and complete communicative tasks.

Xiaoyu's Bonus Content: Nine such sections providing essential cultural and informational points related to each lesson.

Learning Summary: A review and reflection section appearing every three lessons for evaluation of teaching and learning outcomes.

In addition, this book features “Xiaoyu's Tip”, brief notes placed near relevant content to help learners quickly grasp related knowledge.

The book comes with a workbook and a digital edition, accessible via the FLTRP Global Smart Education Platform (https://global.unipus.cn). This platform offers audio, video, interactive modules, and extra exercises. To activate your digital edition, please scan the QR code located on the back cover. Additional teaching and learning resources can be viewed and downloaded on FLTRP CLT Resources (http://www.fltrp-clt.com).

# 内容梗概及主要人物 Content Summary and Main Characters

本册内容继续在海外和中国国内两大场景中展开。故事开始于海外中文学习者白家月与安妮前往北京旅游，描写她们亲身感受到的中国人的热情好客，以及当代中国普通家庭的真实生活。同时，在北京工作的杨同乐到海外探望从事国际中文教育工作的女朋友王一飞，认识了王一飞的学生和朋友，并体会到他们之间互帮互助的温暖情谊。

The content of this volume continues to unfold in two main settings: overseas and in China. The story begins with Bai Jiayue and Annie, overseas learners of Chinese, traveling to Beijing, where they personally experience the hospitality of the Chinese people and the authentic daily life of ordinary contemporary Chinese families. Meanwhile, Yang Tongle, who works in Beijing, goes abroad to visit his girlfriend Wang Yifei, who is engaged in international Chinese language education. During his visit, he meets her friends and students, and experiences the warmth and mutual support in their overseas lives.

#### 中国国内场景：

Domestic Setting in China:

王一雪：女，北京人，30多岁，某跨国公司北京分公司办公室工作人员，王一飞的堂姐。

Wang Yixue: Female, from Beijing, in her thirties, working in the office of the Beijing branch of a multinational company, Wang Yifei's cousin.

刘明：男，北京人，40岁左右，医生，王一雪的丈夫。

Liu Ming: Male, from Beijing, about 40 years old, a doctor, Wang Yixue's husband.

刘小雪：女，北京人，13岁，初中生，刘明和王一雪的女儿。

Liu Xiaoxue: Female, from Beijing, 13 years old, a middle school student, the daughter of Liu Ming and Wang Yixue.

刘小明：男，北京人，6岁，小学生，刘明和王一雪的儿子。

Liu Xiaoming: Male, from Beijing, 6 years old, a primary school student, the son of Liu Ming and Wang Yixue.

Yang Tongle: Male, from Shanghai, in his thirties, a colleague of Wang Yixue. Later, introduced by Wang Yixue, he becomes the boyfriend of Wang Yifei and travels abroad during his vacation to visit her.

# 她请我们吃了北京烤鸭

# She treated us to Peking Duck

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并使用“请”“让”“叫”表达请求。Be able to understand and use“请”，“让”，and “叫” to express requests.

掌握语气助词“吧”（2）的用法，能表达揣测、估计的语气。Master the use of the modal particle“吧”(2) to express a tone of speculation or estimation.

☐ 掌握“是……的”句，能以此强调事情发生的时间、地点、方式等信息。Master the use of the “是……的” sentence to emphasize details such as the time, place, and manner of events.

了解北京名菜烤鸭及其吃法。Learn about Peking Duck, a famous Beijing specialty, and the proper way to enjoy it.

#### 课文1 Text1

在机场，王一雪接到了白家月和安妮。At the airport, Wang Yixue picked up Bai Jiayue and Annie.

A 安妮 B 白家月 C 王一雪

① 就 jiù adv. exactly; precisely

② 给 gěi prep. the recipient of an action) to; for

③ 让 ràng v. let; allow

④ 接 jiē v. meet; welcome; receive

Bai Jiayue: Excuse me, are you Ms. Wang Yifei's cousin?

Wang Yixue: Yes, I am. You two must be her students, right?

Bai Jiayue: Exactly. I'm Bai Jiayue, and this is Annie.

Wang Yixue: Hello, I'm Wang Yixue. Yifei called me and asked me to pick you up.

Bai Jiayue and Annie: Thank you very much.

Wang Yixue: You're welcome.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 语气助词“吧”（2）Modal Particle“吧”(2)

语气助词“吧”用在疑问句末尾，表达揣测、估计的语气。例如：

The modal particle “吧” is used at the end of an interrogative sentence to express a tone of speculation or estimation. For example:

（1）你们就是她的学生吧？（2）她唱歌很好听吧？

（3）陈天中是泰国人吧？

（3）A：很晚了，老师_____？ B：那我们明天再去学校找她。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 “是……的”句“是……的”Sentence

“是……的”句用来强调事情发生的时间、地点、方式、动作者、目的等。肯定句和疑问句中可以省略“是”，否定句中不能省略“是”。例如：

The “是……的” sentence can be used to emphasize the time, place, manner, agent, purpose, etc., of an event. In affirmative and interrogative sentences, “是” can be omitted, but it cannot be omitted in negative sentences. For example:

（1）我们是来旅游的。（2）苹果在哪儿买的？

（3）我们不是坐出租车去的。

Chen Tianzhong: Hello, Jiayue. Do you have time tomorrow? I'd like to ask you for a favor.

Bai Jiayue: Sorry, Tianzhong. I'm already in Beijing.

Chen Tianzhong: When did you get there?

Bai Jiayue: I arrived this morning. If you need anything, you can ask Li Wen for help—he's still at school.

Chen Tianzhong: Okay. Then I'll give him a call.

Bai Jiayue: All right. Goodbye!

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 兼语句 Pivotal Sentences

兼语句的谓语由两个动词短语组成，前一个动词的宾语是第二个动词的主语。前一个动词是“请、让、叫”时，表示让某人做某事。基本结构：主语+请/让/叫+宾语（→主语）+动词或动词性短语。例如：

A pivotal sentence consists of two verbal phrases, in which the object of the first verb serves as the subject of the second verb. When the preceding verb is “请”, “让”, or “叫”, it indicates asking or having someone do something. Basic structure: Subject+请/让/叫+Object ( $ \rightarrow $Subject)+Verb or Verbal Phrase. For example:

（1）我想请你帮个忙。（2）王老师让我们说中文。

（3）妈妈叫孩子们回家。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：明天我想请 来 ___。

B：好的，我现在就去找她。

（3）A：小雪，你去哪儿？

B: 我去超市。妈妈叫_____。

此处“多听、多说”表示反复多次地听和说。Here，“多听、多说”denotes repeated listening and speaking.

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

两人一组，一人来火车站接朋友，一人刚出站、是接站人的朋友。接站人向朋友询问这里的目的和现在要去的地方，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. One student plays the person picking up a friend at the train station, and the other plays the friend who has just got out of the station. The student picking up the friend asks about the purpose of the visit, the place the friend is going to, and other related information. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并介绍学校的基本情况。Be able to understand and talk about basic information about a school.

☐ 能听懂并使用“多”表达大概的数量。Be able to understand and use“多”to express approximate quantities.

☐ 掌握固定格式“还是……吧”的用法，能表达选择。Master the use of the fixed pattern“还是……吧”to express choices.

了解中国著名学府——北京大学。Learn about Peking University, a prestigious university in China.

##### 生词 New Words (2-2)

① 公交车 gōngjiāochē n. bus

② 但 dàn conj. but; yet

③ 车站 chēzhàn n. stop; station

④ 远 yuǎn adj. far away

⑤ 打车 dǎchē v. take/hail a taxi

⑥ 还是 háishi adv. had better

北京大学Běijīng Dàxué Peking University

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1fbf4597-662d-46ab-98d1-246224d3f045/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_290_1295_365_1408.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A20Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb656e90164d22dd2266d6560b4a887d7747bc838b1d9a109ad54836027bca76b" alt="Image" width="6%" /></div>


这里 “好” 的意思是 “容易”。Here, “好” means “easy”.

Bai Jiayue: Excuse me, is there a bus from here to Peking University?

Receptionist: Yes, but the bus stop is a bit far.

Bai Jiayue: Is it easy to get a taxi around here?

Receptionist: Yes, it is.

Bai Jiayue: Thank you. Annie, let's take a taxi instead.

Annie: Okay, no problem.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 固定格式“还是……吧” Fixed Pattern “还是……吧”

固定格式“还是……吧”表示倾向性选择，有“这么办比较好”的意思。例如：

The fixed pattern “还是……吧” is used to express a preferential choice, with the connotation that “this way is better”. For example:

（1）我们还是打车去吧。（2）那件衣服很好看，还是买那件吧。

（3）你第一次去北京，还是找个人接你吧。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：今天你怎么去公司？ B：打车太贵了，___。

（2）A：不好意思，我今天太忙了，你 $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $。

B：好的，我们去找天中帮忙。

（3）A：明天有雨，我们 B：好的，那就今天去。

used at the end of a sentence to express appreciation, agreement, etc.

used at the end of a sentence to express appreciation, agreement, etc.

⑧万 wàn num. ten thousand

⑨ 名 míng m./n. place in a competition; name

⑩ 网上 wǎngshang n. online

⑪ 外国 wàiguó n. foreign country

⑫ 间 jiān m. used of smallest units of housing

Annie: Yeah! Peking University has over 40,000 students.

Bai Jiayue: How do you know?

Annie: I read it online. It also says there are more than 3000 international students at Peking University.

Bai Jiayue: I'd love to study here too.

Annie: There's a classroom right over there. Let's go and have a look.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

2 用 “多” 表达概数 Expressing Approximate Numbers with “多”

“多”用在数词或数量词后，表示有零头。当数字是10的整数倍时，“多”一般用在数词后面；当数字不是10的整数倍时，“多”一般用在量词后面。例如：

Adding “多” after a number or a numeral phrase indicates that the quantity is slightly greater than the stated number. When the number is a multiple of ten, “多” is usually placed directly after the numeral. When the number is not a multiple of ten, “多” is usually placed after the measure word. For example:

（1）北京大学有四万多名学生呢！

（2）教室里有二十多个学生。

（3）这两个苹果五块多钱。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 动词或动词性短语、主谓短语作定语 Verbs, Verbal Phrases, and Subject-Predicate Phrases as Attributives

动词或动词性短语、主谓短语用在名词前面，表示名词的特征或状态。例如：

Verbs, verbal phrases, or subject-predicate phrases can be placed before a noun to indicate the characteristics or state of that noun. For example:

（1）他们卖的电影票也很便宜。（2）现在学中文的学生很多。

（3）这是朋友给我的杯子。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

两人一组，一人问路，一人回答。问路的人询问附近有没有到新新电影院的公交车，车站远不远，到电影院要多长时间，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. One student asks for directions, and the other answers. The student asking for directions asks questions such as whether there is a bus nearby to Xinxin Cinema, whether the bus stop is far, and how long it takes to get to the cinema. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

# 我想去西安旅游

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并询问事情的原因。Be able to understand and ask about the reasons for something.

掌握结果补语的用法，能描述动作的结果。Master the use of the complement of result to describe the results of actions.

掌握动词重叠的用法，能用来表达动作时间短、数量少、尝试等意义。Master the use of verb reduplication to express brief actions, limited repetitions, or simple attempts.

了解西安的旅游景点。Learn about the tourist attractions in Xi'an.

#### 热身 Warm-Up

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 结果补语 Complement of Result

一些动词或者形容词用在动词后面，表示动作的结果。例如：

Some verbs or adjectives can be placed after a verb to indicate the result of the action. For example:

（1）菜都做好了。（2）我吃完饭了。

（3）小雪今天来晚了。

否定形式是在动词前面加“没（有）”，同时要去掉“了”。例如：

The negative form is formed by adding “没（有）” before the verb and removing “了”. For example:

（1）我没吃完饭。（2）小雪没来晚。

疑问形式有三种，分别是：（1）在句尾加“了吗”；（2）在句尾加“（了）没有”；（3）动词+没+动词+结果补语。例如：

There are three types of interrogative forms: (1) add “了吗” at the end of the sentence; (2) add “(了)没有” at the end of the sentence; and (3) Verb+没+Verb+Complement of Result. For example:

（1）你吃完饭了吗？（2）小雪来晚了没有？

（3）你学没学会？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

(1) A: 小明，你___衣服没有？ B: 我穿好了。

（2）A：老师，我写完了。 B：很好，这几个字都了。（对）

（3）A：这本书你_____？ B：没看懂，我有很多问题。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 动词重叠（1） Verb Reduplication (1)

动作性比较强、能重复或持续的动词重叠使用，表示时间短、数量少、尝试等意思，语气比较轻松、随意，多用于口语。

单音节动词的重叠形式是“A（一）A”，双音节动词的重叠形式是“ABAB”，离合词的重叠形式是“AAB”。例如：

Action verbs that are dynamic, repeatable or continuous may appear in a reduplicated form. Such reduplication usually indicates brief actions, limited repetitions, or simple attempts. It conveys a relaxed and casual tone and is mostly used in spoken Chinese.

The reduplicated form of a monosyllabic verb is “A (—) A”; that of a disyllabic verb is “ABAB”; and that of a separable word is “AAB”. For example:

（1）那你再想一想，你想好了，我来买票。

（2）我有点儿累，现在想休息休息。

（3）你能过来帮帮忙吗？

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//1b98d9d7-267f-4688-9a12-045fe6fc2842/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_549_494_626_606.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A21Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb327444b5b784e73022a15123c5ca8cf7a6ffc17a41ac242dac99b9c21ecb449" alt="Image" width="6%" /></div>


“对了”表示突然想起另外一件事或提出另一个话题。“对了”is used when you suddenly remember something else or bring up another topic.

Liu Ming: Have an apple. I've already washed them.

Wang Yixue: Okay.

Liu Ming: They're on the table. Help yourself.

Wang Yixue: I'm going to wash my hands first. By the way, how about taking a trip to Xi'an?

Liu Ming: Why do you want to go to Xi'an?

Wang Yixue: I read some introductions

online—it's a great time to visit Xi'an!

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 动词重叠（2）Verb Reduplication (2)

表达已经发生的情况时，单音节动词的重叠形式是“A了A”；双音节动词一般不能用重叠形式，只能用“AB了一下”；离合词的重叠形式是“A了AB”。例如：

When describing an action that has already taken place, the reduplicated form of a monosyllabic verb is “A了A”. Generally, a disyllabic verb cannot be used in its reduplicated form; instead,

“AB了一下” is used. The reduplicated form of a separable word is “A了AB”. For example:

（1）我看了看网上的介绍。（2）我休息了一下，现在觉得不累了。

（3）他昨天来帮了帮忙。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

(1) A: 昨天你在家吗? B: 我有点儿事,  $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $ 了一下。

（2）A：你的手机是不是在桌子上？B：我在桌子上___，但没有。

（3）A：昨天下午你做什么了？ B：我在房间 $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $书， $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $字。

早上，刘明开车送孩子去学校，送完孩子回家后，医院就来电话了，让他回去上班。我觉得他这个月每天都很累，真想让他休息休息。

##### 生词 New Words (1) 3-8

①2 送 sòng v. accompany sb to his destination; give (as a gift)

回回去 huíqù v. return; go back

每 měi pron. each; every

累 lèi adj. tired; weary

In the morning, Liu Ming drove the child to school. But just after he got home, the hospital called—they asked him to go back to work. I think he's been very tired every day this month. I really wish he could get some rest.

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，互相询问：想不想去旅游？想好去哪儿旅游了吗？为什么选择该旅游地？等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

# 你穿红色的很好看

##### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并询问过去的经历。Be able to understand and ask about past experiences.

☐ 能听懂并使用描述的方式指称事物。Be able to understand and refer to things through description.

掌握因果复句“因为……，所以……”的用法，能表达事情的原因和结果。Master the use of the causal complex sentence“因为……，所以……”to express causes and results of events.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 动态助词“过” Aspect Particle“过”

动态助词“过”用在动词后面，表示动作曾在过去发生，但未持续到现在。基本结构：主语+动词+过+宾语。否定形式是在动词前面加“没（有）”。例如：

The aspect particle “过” is used after a verb to indicate that an action occurred in the past but has not continued to the present. Basic structure: Subject+Verb+过+Object. The negative form is constructed by adding “没（有）” before the verb.

（1）她去过中国。（2）我吃过饺子，很好吃。

（3）她没去过中国。

疑问形式有三种：（1）在句尾加“吗”；（2）在句尾加“没有”；（3）动词+没+动词+过。例如：

There are three types of interrogative forms: (1) add “吗” at the end of the sentence; (2) add “没有” at the end of the sentence; and (3) Verb+没+Verb+过. For example:

（1）我们来过这家商场吗？（2）陈天中吃过饺子没有？

（3）你看没看过那个电影？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：今天晚上我想看个电影。

B：这个电影不错，，你也看看吧。

（2）A：这家超市的苹果好吃吗？ B：，我也不知道。

（3）A： $ \underline{\hspace{2em}} $北京没有？ B： $ \underline{\hspace{2em}} $。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

2 因果复句“因为……，所以……” Causal Complex Sentence“因为……，所以……”

“因为……所以……”构成因果关系复句。“因为”和“所以”可以成对使用，也可以只用其中的一个。例如：

The structure “因为……所以……” forms a complex sentence expressing cause and effect. “因为” and “所以” can be used either together as a pair or individually. For example:

（1）就是因为没穿过，所以要试试啊！

（2）因为我生病了，今天没去上班。

（3）我没去过他家，所以让他来车站接我。

Liu Xiaoxue: Mom, I want to buy a new schoolbag.

Wang Yixue: Okay. They're selling schoolbags over there. Let's go and take a look.

Liu Xiaoxue: There are so many beautiful ones!

Wang Yixue: Red ones, green ones, black ones—which one do you like?

Liu Xiaoxue: The green one.

Wang Yixue: Not bad. I think the green one looks better too.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 “的”字短语 “的” Phrase

结构助词“的”用在名词、代词、动词、形容词等后面，组成“的”字短语，相当于名词性短语。例如：

The structural particle “的” is used after nouns, pronouns, verbs, adjectives, etc., to form a “的” phrase, which functions as a noun phrase. For example:

（1）红色的、绿色的、黑色的，你想买哪个？（=红色的书包、绿色的书包、黑色的书包）

（2）这个面包是爸爸买的，妈妈买的在那儿。（=买的面包）

（3）这件衣服太贵了，还是买那件便宜的吧。（=便宜的衣服）

（3）A：今天的饭菜是爸爸做的，好吃吗？

B：好吃，但我更喜欢吃妈妈 ___。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

两人一组，一人扮演售货员，一人扮演顾客。顾客想买裤子，询问裤子的颜色、大小和价钱，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. One student plays the salesperson and the other plays the customer. The customer wants to buy a pair of pants and asks about the colors, sizes, prices, and other details. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

# Dì-yī cì qu Zhōngguó péngyou jiā

# 第一次去中国朋友家

# Visiting a Chinese friend's home for the first time

##### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用趋向补语表达动作的方向。Be able to understand and use complements of direction to express the direction of an action.

掌握固定格式“都……了”，能表达已经或者达到的意思。Master the use of the fixed pattern “都……了” to express the meaning of “already” or “up to a certain point”.

了解中国人见面时的称呼方式。Understand how Chinese people address each other when they meet.

Annie: Jiayue, come down quickly. It's our first time visiting a Chinese friend's home. Don't be late.

Bai Jiayue: We still have time. Why don't you come up?

Annie: I'm not going up. I'll wait for you downstairs.

Bai Jiayue: Okay, I'll come down in a few minutes.

Annie: Hurry up.

Bai Jiayue: It's fine. Sister Yixue said we just need to get there before 11 o'clock.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 简单趋向补语（1） Simple Complement of Direction (1)

简单趋向补语的基本结构是“动词+来/去”。其中，“来”表示动作朝着说话人的方向进行，“去”表示动作背离说话人的方向进行，最常用的动词有“上、下、进、出（chū，get out）、回、过”。例如：

The basic structure of simple complements of direction is “Verb+来/去”. Here, “来” indicates that the action is moving toward the speaker, while “去” indicates that the action is moving away from the speaker. In this structure, the most commonly used verbs include “上”, “下”, “进”, “出”, “回”, and “过”. For example:

（1）我不上去了，就在下面等你。（说话人在下边，听话人在上边）

（2）家月到下边了，你下去接她吧。（说话人在上边，听话人在上边）

（3）我在外边呢，你出来（chūlái, come out）吧。（说话人在外边，听话人在里边）

另外，“叫、开、买、拿、请、送、要、找、走”等动词也可以用在这个结构中。例如：

Additionally, verbs such as “叫”, “开”, “买”, “拿”, “请”, “送”, “要”, “找”, and “走” can also be used in this structure. For example:

（1）她拿来一本中文书。（2）你别过来拿了，我给你送去。

“起来（qǐlái，get up）”是一种比较特殊的用法，它表示起床或动作向上的趋向。例如：

“起来” is a special expression in Chinese that can either mean “to get up (from bed)”, or indicate an upward directional movement. For example:

（1）今天早上5点她就起来了。（2）你起来吧，让小妹妹坐这儿。

##### 完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：这就是我家， $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $看看吧。 B：你家真漂亮！

#### 语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 简单趋向补语（2） Simple Complement of Direction (2)

动词既带简单趋向补语，又带宾语时，如果宾语是地点名词，放在“来/去”前面；宾语是事物名词，放在“来/去”前后都可以。例如：

When a verb takes both a simple complement of direction and an object: if the object is a location noun, place it before “来/去”; if the object is a thing noun, it can be placed either before or after “来/去”. For example:

（1）你们太客气了，还拿这么多礼物来！（2）家月给我送来了一本书。

（3）上课了，你们快进教室来吧。

另外，动词“开、买、拿、请、送、要、找、走”等也可以加上“上、下、进出、回、过”等简单趋向补语。这种情况下，动词一般带宾语，并且放在“上、下、进、出、回、过”后面。例如：

In addition, verbs such as “开”，“买”，“拿”，“请”，“送”，“要”，“找”，and “走” can also combine with complements of direction like “上”，“下”，“进”，“出”，“回”，and “过”. In this case, the verb usually takes an object, which is placed after “上”，“下”，“进”，“出”，“回”，and “过”. For example:

（1）爸爸今天买回了很多水果。

（2）妈妈拿出了二十块钱，让小雪自己去买点儿吃的。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：小李没来吃饭吗？B：他吃完了，已经 $ \underline{公司} $了。（回）

(2) A: 这么多水果是谁的? (送)

B：家月和李文。你现在想吃吗？我去洗一洗。

(3) A: 你看见天中了吗? B: 我看见他 $ \underline{\text{书店了。(走)}} $

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 固定格式“都……了” Fixed Pattern“都……了”

固定格式“都……了”表示已经、达到，一般含有强调或者不满的语气。例如：

The fixed pattern “都……了” conveys meanings such as “already” or “up to a certain point”, and generally carries a tone of emphasis or dissatisfaction. For example:

（1）都12点了，我们吃饭吧。（2）都8点半了，你还不起床吗？

（3）我都去过北京了，不想再去了。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：_____，我们快走吧。 B：没事，9点前到就不晚。

（2）A：你这么晚回来，我们 ___。B：没关系，我在公司吃饭了。

（3）A：你的病___，为什么还不去上班？B：我想再休息休息。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

四人一组，一人扮演主人，两人扮演家庭成员，一人扮演客人。客人来家里做客，大家互相打招呼，主人给家里人介绍客人，主人招待客人，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in groups of four. One student plays the host, two play family members, and one plays the guest. The guest comes to visit the host's home. Everyone exchanges greetings; the host introduces the guest to the family members and entertains the guest, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

# 小雪，生日快乐！

Happy birthday, Xiaoxue!

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用形容词重叠形式描述性质或状态。Be able to understand and use reduplicated adjectives to describe qualities or states.

掌握固定短语“什么的”的用法，能表达列举未尽的意思。Master the use of the set phrase “什么的” to express incomplete enumeration.

掌握结构助词“地”的用法，能描述动作进行的方式或状态。Master the use of the structural particle“地”to describe the manner or state in which an action is carried out.

了解中国人庆祝生日的习俗。Understand how Chinese people celebrate birthdays and their related customs.

Wang Yixue: Tomorrow is our daughter's birthday.

Liu Ming: I would have totally forgotten if you hadn't reminded me. What should we get her as a gift?

Wang Yixue: She likes drawing. How about some color pens?

Liu Ming: Let's get her color pens!

Wang Yixue: I'll go buy them tomorrow morning.

Liu Ming: Sure! I'll also get her a big birthday cake.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 形容词重叠 Adjective Reduplication

单音节形容词 “A” 的重叠形式为 “AA”，双音节形容词 “AB” 的重叠形式一般为 “AABB”。形容词重叠表示程度深或者表达喜爱的情感。例如：

The reduplicated form of a monosyllabic adjective “A” is “AA”, while the reduplicated form of a disyllabic adjective “AB” is generally “AABB”. Adjective reduplication is often used to intensify the degree of the adjective or to express feelings of fondness or affection. For example:

（1）我再给她买个大大的生日蛋糕。

（2）这只猫小小的，真让人喜欢。

（3）一雪的女儿每天都漂漂亮亮的。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：今天买的苹果 ___。 B：我吃了一个，也很好吃。

（2）A：这家饭店的饺子 ___ ，我一次能吃二十个。

B：吃吧，我知道你爱吃饺子。

（3）A：这几天你在家 $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $休息，别出去了。

B：好的，我也觉得太累了。

Liu Ming: Happy birthday, Xiaoxue!

Liu Xiaoming: Happy birthday, sister!

Wang Yixue: Xiaoxue, here's a gift from dad and mom.

Liu Ming: Go ahead and open it—see if you like it.

Liu Xiaoxue: Color pens! I love them!

Wang Yixue: What would you like to draw?

Liu Xiaoxue: Our home! With dad, mom, my little brother, and our black dog, white cat, and all the others.

Liu Xiaoming: Then I'll draw a sister in white clothes.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 固定短语 “什么的” Set Phrase “什么的”

固定短语 “什么的” 表示 “……之类” 的意思。基本结构：……什么的。例如：

The set phrase “什么的” indicates “and so on” or “and the like”. Basic structure: ……什么的. For example:

（1）画我们的家！有爸爸、妈妈、弟弟，还有黑色的狗、白色的猫什么的。

（2）桌子上有电脑、杯子、书和画笔什么的。

（3）她拿来了一些水、面包和苹果什么的。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你爱吃什么？ B：_____，我都爱吃。

（2）A：那间教室里有些什么？ B：_____。

（3）A：休息的时候，你喜欢做些什么？B：___。

#### 课文3 Text3

在客厅，刘明一家人在给刘小雪过生日。In the living room, Liu Ming and his family were celebrating Liu Xiaoxue's birthday.

听两遍对话，选择正确答案。Listen to the dialogue twice and choose the correct answer. 6-5

（1）今天的东西都是谁爱吃的？（ ）

A 爸爸 B 妈妈 C 小雪

（2）他们吃完饭要做什么？（___）

A 休息 B 买蛋糕 C 出去玩

中国人过生日的时候一般要吃面条儿，因为面条儿长长的，寓意“长寿”。When celebrating birthdays, Chinese people usually eat noodles, as their length symbolizes longevity.

Xiǎoxuě. kànkan jīntiān yǒu shénme

hǎochī de.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 结构助词“地” Structural Particle“地”

结构助词“地”一般用在形容词和动词中间，表示动作行为的状态或方式。例如：

The structural particle “地” is generally placed between an adjective and a verb to indicate the manner or state in which an action is performed. For example:

（1）过生日就要吃好吃的，还要高高兴兴地玩。

（2）老师早早地到了教室。

（3）爸爸很快地吃完早饭，就去上班了。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你姐姐去哪儿了？B：她生病了，回房间休息了。（早）

（2）A：你回家 $ \underline{\text{}} $睡一觉，明天也别来上班了。（好）

B：好的，您也别太累了。

（3）A：小明喜欢你送的礼物吗？

B: 喜欢，他拿到礼物_____说：“这个礼物太好了！”（高兴）

（1）女儿学会做面条儿了。（）

（2）王一雪今天高高兴兴地过了个生日。（）

Jintian shì nổ'ér de shēngrì. Wômen mãile

今天是女儿的生日。我们买了

dàngāo, zuòle miàntiáor, hái zuòle yú a ròu

蛋糕，做了面条儿，还做了鱼啊肉

a shénmede. Chīwán wǎnfàn, yì jiā rén qù

啊什么的。吃完晚饭，一家人去

kànle gè diànyǐng. Huí jiā hòu, háizimen zǎozǎo

看了个电影。回家后，孩子们早早

de jiù shàng chuáng le. Míngtiān bú shàngxué. tāmen shuō yào shūshūfúfú de shuì yí jiào. ràng

地就上床了。明天不上学，他们说要舒舒服服地睡一觉，让

wǒmen wǎn diǎnr jiào tāmen qǐchuáng. Zhè shì hěn máng, hěn lèi, dàn hěn kuàilè de yì tiān.

我们晚点儿叫他们起床。这是很忙、很累，但很快乐的一天。

 $ ^{13} $床 chuáng n. bed

④ 舒服shūfu adj. comfortable

Today was our daughter's birthday. We bought a cake, made noodles, and prepared dishes such as fish, meat, and others. After dinner, our whole family went to watch a movie. When we got home, the children went to bed early. Since there's no school tomorrow, they said they wanted to sleep soundly and comfortably, and asked us to wake them up later. It was a busy and tiring day, but full of joy.

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

三人一组，一人扮演过生日的人，两人扮演朋友，三人一起庆祝生日。朋友们说生日祝福、送礼物、问生日愿望。过生日的人表达想吃什么、想去哪儿玩，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in groups of three. One student plays the birthday person, and the other two play friends. The three celebrate the birthday together. The friends give birthday wishes, present gifts, and ask about the birthday person's wishes. The birthday person talks about what they'd like to eat, where they want to go, and other ideas. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并描述动作进行的状态。Be able to understand and describe the state of an ongoing action.

□ 掌握紧缩复句“一……就……”的用法，能表达动作接连发生的意思。Master the use of the contracted complex sentence “一……就……”to express the meaning that actions occur in succession.

☐ 能谈论常做的运动。Be able to talk about regular physical activities.

在教室，安妮和陈天中在聊天儿。In the classroom, Annie and Chen Tianzhong were chatting.

（1）安妮是什么时候回来的？（ ）

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

1 紧缩复句 “—……就……” Contracted Complex Sentence “—……就……”

紧缩复句 “一……就……” 表示后一动作紧跟着前一动作发生，也表示前一动作是条件和原因，后一动作是结果。主语相同时，主语在 “一” 或 “就” 前面；主语不同时，两个主语分别在 “一” 和 “就” 前面。例如：

The contracted complex sentence “—……就……” indicates that the second action occurs immediately after the first; it also means the first action serves as the condition or cause, and the second as the result. When the subjects are the same, the subject is placed before “—” or “就”; when the subjects are different, the two subjects are placed before “—” and “就” respectively. For example:

（1）你怎么一下课就往外跑？

（2）我一到家，妈妈就打来电话了。

（3）一到星期六，陈天中就跟同学去打篮球。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你今天早点儿回来。 B：好的，我就回来。

（2）A：我一下飞机，___。B：因为我给她打电话了，让她去接你。

（3）A：你看，小猫_____就过来了。

B：是啊，因为它爱吃鱼。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 状态补语（1）Complement of State (1)

状态补语是在动词后边补充说明动作进行的状态的。基本结构：动词+得+形容词性短语。否定形式是在形容词的前面加“不”。例如：

The complement of state follows a verb to supplement the state of the action. Basic structure: Verb+得+Adjectival Phrase. The negative form is to add “不” before the adjective. For example:

（1）我踢得还可以。

（2）他们玩得很高兴。

（3）白家月跑得不快。

疑问形式有三种：（1）在句尾加“吗”；（2）动词+得+形容词+不+形容词；

（3）动词+得+怎么样。例如：

There are three interrogative forms: (1) add “吗” at the end of the sentence; (2) Verb+得+Adjective+不+Adjective; and (3) Verb+得+怎么样. For example:

（2）他来得早不早？

（1）你跑得快吗？

（3）他们玩得怎么样？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你听，她唱得怎么样？

B：___。

（2）A：妹妹喜欢吃这个菜吗？

B：不太喜欢，她___。

（3）A：你昨天晚上_____？（睡）

B：_____。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 状态补语（2）Complement of State (2)

带状态补语的句子中，如果动词是离合词，需要重复动词性语素。如果动词有宾语，可以把宾语提前，或者重复动词。例如：

In sentences with a complement of state: if the verb is a separable word, the verbal morpheme needs to be repeated; if the verb takes an object, the object can be placed before the verb or the verb can be repeated. For example:

（1）我游泳游得不快。

（2）你篮球打得怎么样？

（3）白家月写汉字写得很好看。

听两遍课文，判断正误。Listen to the text twice and decide if the statements are true or false. 7-7

（1）陈天中非常喜欢运动。（）（2）陈天中游泳游得不快。（）

我的爱好是运动。从上小学开始，我每天都跟爸爸去运动。现在我篮球打得很好，足球踢得不错，游泳游得也很快。我一有时间就去运动。

生词 New Words (♩) 7-8

爱好 àihào n./v. hobby; be fond of

①开始 kāishǐ v./n. start; beginning

My hobby is doing sports. Since primary school, I’ve been exercising with my dad every day. Now I play basketball very well, play football quite well, and swim very fast. Whenever I have time, I go out to play sports.

朗读课文，读后选择正确答案。Read the text and then choose the correct answer.

（1）陈天中篮球打得怎么样？（ ）

A 很好 B 不好 C 还可以

（2）陈天中一有时间就做什么？（___）

A 运动 B 休息 C 跟爸爸出去

# 虽然你忘了，但是我记得

Even though you forgot, I remembered

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并谈论生日的庆祝活动。Be able to understand and talk about birthday celebrations.

能听懂并使用“比”描述事物之间的差别。Be able to understand and use“比”to describe differences between things.

掌握转折复句“虽然……，但是……”的用法，能表达语义的转折。Master the use of the adversative complex sentence“虽然……，但是……”to express semantic contrast.

了解中国人对一些数字的喜好。Understand Chinese people's preferences for certain numbers.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 比较句（1） Comparative Sentences (1)

本课学习用介词“比”来比较性质和状态差别的比较句。基本结构：A 比 B+形容词或形容词性短语。例如：

In this lesson, we learn to use the preposition “比” in comparative sentences to compare differences in nature and state. Basic structure: A 比 B+ Adjective or Adjectival Phrase. For example:

（1）我也觉得左边的比右边的好看。

（2）今天比昨天冷。

（3）他觉得踢足球比打篮球有意思。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：这家饭店有包子、饺子和面条儿。你想吃什么？

B：饺子吧，我觉得。

（2）A：这家书店 ___。

B：对，前边那家书店太小了。

（3）A：这条黑色的裤子 ___。

B：是，我也觉得有点儿长，还是买那条白色的吧。

Liu Ming: There are quite a few movies showing today. Let's watch one.

Wang Yixue: Great! Which one should we watch?

Liu Ming: I remember you like romantic movies. How about that romantic one?

Wang Yixue: Let's watch this one instead. I read online that it's more interesting than that romantic one.

Liu Ming: Okay. I'll go buy the tickets.

Wang Yixue: Buy them online. It's cheaper to buy tickets online than here.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 比较句（2） Comparative Sentences (2)

在用 “比” 的比较句中，形容词前不能用 “很” “非常” 等程度副词，可以用 “还” 和 “更” 表示程度加深。例如：

In comparative sentences with “比”, adverbs of degree such as “很” and “非常” cannot be used before the adjective, but “还” and “更” can be used to enhance the degree. For example:

（1）我看网上说这个电影比那个爱情片更有意思。（爱情片很有意思，这个电影更有意思）

（2）今天比昨天更热。（昨天很热，今天更热）

（3）我觉得奶茶比牛奶还好喝。（我觉得牛奶很好喝，奶茶更好喝）

Liu Ming: Happy birthday! Even though you forgot, I remembered. Look, what's this?

Liu Ming: Although we spent some money, we celebrated a happy birthday.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 转折复句“虽然……，但是……” Adversative Complex Sentence“虽然……，但是……”

“虽然……，但是……”构成转折复句，“虽然”和“但是”可以成对使用，也可以只用其中一个。例如：

“虽然……，但是……” forms an adversative complex sentence. The two words can be used in pairs or singly. For example:

（1）虽然你忘了，但是我记得。（2）外边下雪了，但是不太冷。

（3）虽然觉得有点儿累，我还是走回家了。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-Play

三人一组，一人扮演服务员，另外两人扮演客人。客人来饭馆吃饭。点菜时，他们询问菜的价格、味道，并对比饭馆的菜和自己家里做的菜，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in groups of three. One student plays the waiter/waitress, and the other two play customers. The customers come to the restaurant for a meal. While ordering, they ask about the prices and tastes of the dishes, compare the restaurant's food with the dishes they cook at home, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并使用“没有”描述事物之间的差别。Be able to understand and use“没有”to describe differences between things.

☐ 掌握动词“离”的用法，能表达处所或时间的距离。Master the use of the verb“离”to express spatial or temporal distance.

☐ 掌握时量补语（1）的用法，能描述动作持续的时间。Master the use of the complement of duration (1) to describe how long an action lasts.

这里“会”是“可能”的意思。Here, “会” expresses the meaning of possibility.

Wang Yixue: Our son's pants are torn. Let's buy him a new pair.

Liu Ming: Okay.

Wang Yixue: How about this pair of black ones?

Liu Ming: They're not as nice as the ones you bought last time.

Wang Yixue: The boy next to us is wearing pants like these, and I think they look great!

Liu Ming: Our son isn't as tall as him, so they wouldn't look so good on him.

Wang Yixue: Alright, let's go over there and take another look.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 比较句（3） Comparative Sentences (3)

本课学习用“没有”表示比较的比较句，意思是“不如”“不及”。基本结构：A没有B+形容词或形容词性短语。形容词前面可以加“这么”或者“那么”，表示B的程度更高。例如：

In this lesson, we learn to use “没有” in comparative sentences to express the meaning of “not as...as” or “inferior to”. Basic structure: A没有B+Adjective or Adjectival Phrase. The adjective can be preceded by “这么” or “那么” to indicate that B has a higher degree. For example:

（1）儿子的个子没有他那么高。（2）昨天没有今天这么冷。

（3）这块手表没有那块好看。

“没有”的肯定形式“有”常用在表示比较的疑问句中。例如：

The affirmative form of “没有”, which is “有”, is often used in interrogative sentences to indicate comparison. For example:

（1）妹妹有姐姐高吗？（2）那件衣服有这件好看吗？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：我女儿今年六岁多了，你儿子几岁了？

B：四岁多，没有 ___。

（2）A：这件黑色的衣服好看吗？

B：好看，但_____，还是买那件白色的吧。

(3) A: 今天_____昨天那么_____吗?

B: 没有，我觉得昨天更热。

Wang Yixue: There's a bubble tea shop at the entrance. Do you want a cup of bubble tea?

Liu Ming: I'd prefer coffee. Let's go to a coffee shop.

Wang Yixue: The coffee shop is a bit far from here.

Liu Ming: That's fine. The coffee in that shop is very good.

Wang Yixue: Then wait here for a moment while I go and buy a cup of bubble tea.

Liu Ming: Don't you want to drink coffee?

Wang Yixue: I can't fall asleep if I drink coffee.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 动词“离” Verb“离”

动词“离”表示处所、时间的距离。基本结构：A离B……。例如：

The verb “离” is used to indicate spatial or temporal distance. Basic structure: A 离B……. For example:

（1）咖啡店离这儿有点儿远。（2）学校离医院不远。

（3）现在离我的生日还有三天。

（3）A：现在  $ \underline{\hspace{2cm}} $ 还有一个小时。

B：那想想下班后我们去哪儿吃饭吧。

##### 生词 New Words (♩) 9-6

⑪ 近

close; nearby

 $ ^{12} $走路 zǒulù v. walk

Liu Ming: Let's take a taxi home.

Wang Yixue: It's close to home. Let's walk.

Liu Ming: How long will it take?

Wang Yixue: Walking will take a little more than half an hour.

Liu Ming: Alright. I take the car to and from work every day, so I'll get some exercise today.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 时量补语（1）Complement of Duration (1)

表示时间段的词语用在动词后面构成时量补语，说明动作或状态持续的时间。基本结构：主语+动词+时量补语。例如：

Time expressions are used after verbs to form complements of duration, indicating the length of time an action or a state lasts. Basic structure: Subject+Verb+Complement of Duration. For example:

（1）走半个多小时就到了。（2）他们学了两年。

##### （3）我们休息十分钟。

动词后带宾语时，宾语的位置有两种情况：（1）宾语是事物名词，一般在时量补语后；（2）宾语是称呼或代词，一般在时量补语前。例如：

When a verb is followed by an object, the position of the object can be in two cases: (1) if the object is a thing noun, it is usually placed after the complement of duration; (2) if the object is a term of address or a pronoun, it is usually placed before the complement of duration. For example:

（1）我看了一个晚上电视。（2）李文等了她一个小时。

##### （3）我找了陈天中二十多分钟。

##### 动词带宾语时，也可以重复动词后再加时量补语。例如：

When a verb is followed by an object, the verb can be repeated before adding the complement of duration. For example:

（1）他们学中文学了两年。（2）李文等她等了一个小时。

动词是离合词时，需要重复动词性语素，再加时量补语。例如：

When the verb is a separable word, the verbal morpheme needs to be repeated before adding the complement of duration. For example:

（1）安妮游泳游了一个下午。（2）陈天中跑步跑了两个小时。

##### 动词后有“了”，句尾还有语气助词“了”，表示动作仍在进行。例如：

When “了” follows the verb and another “了” appears at the end of the sentence as a modal particle, it indicates that the action is still ongoing. For example:

（1）他写了半个小时汉字了。（他现在还在写汉字。）

（2）陈天中跑步跑了两个小时了。（陈天中还在跑步。）

##### 完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：儿子今天出去打了 ___。（一下午）

B：这是他的爱好，一有时间就去打球。

（2）A：你___，我去给你拿杯水来。（一会儿）

B：你别忙了，我说完话就回去了。

（3）A：你的小猫找到了吗？

B：没有，我都找___。（一天）

两人一组，商量一起旅游的出行方式，比较一下不同出行方式的优缺点。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用主谓谓语句对人、事物或情况进行说明。Be able to understand and use sentences with a subject-predicate phrase as the predicate to describe people, things, or situations.

☐ 能听懂并使用选择问句表示疑问。Be able to understand and use alternative questions to express inquiries.

掌握固定格式“要/快/快要/就要……了”的用法，能表达某事即将发生。Master the use of the fixed pattern“要/快/快要/就要……了”to express that something is about to happen.

##### 生词 New Words (10-2)

① 开学 kāixué v. school) begin

② 门 mén n. door

③ 后面 hòu miàn n. back

④ 笔 bǐ n. pen; pencil

⑤ 帮 bāng v. help

Liu Ming: Xiaoming, school will start tomorrow. Are you ready?

Liu Xiaoming: School starts tomorrow? Dad, did you see my schoolbag?

Liu Ming: It's behind the door.

Liu Xiaoming: Where are my books? And my pens?

Liu Ming: The books are on the bed, and the pens are on the table.

Liu Xiaoming: Great! Everything is ready now.

Liu Ming: Dad helped you this time. Next time, you prepare it by yourself, okay?

Liu Xiaoming: Okay!

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 主谓谓语句 Sentences with a Subject-Predicate Phrase as the Predicate

主谓短语可以作谓语，对主语加以说明或描写，构成主谓谓语句。一般来说，主谓短语中的主语是全句主语的一部分或跟它相关。基本结构：主语+主谓短语。例如：

A subject-predicate phrase can function as a predicate, providing an explanation or description of the subject, thereby forming a sentence with a subject-predicate phrase as the predicate. Generally speaking, the subject in the subject-predicate phrase is either a part of the whole sentence's subject or related to it. Basic structure: Subject+Subject-Predicate Phrase. For example:

（1）我的书包你看见了吗？

（2）弟弟手很小。

（3）这件事他知道。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：刘明工作忙吗？

B： $ \underline{\text{}} $。

（2）A：你知道哪个电影好看吗？

B: 这个电影_____，很好看。

（3）A：陈天中学习怎么样？

B：___。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 选择问句 Alternative Questions

连词“还是”用在疑问句中，表示选择。基本结构：（是）A还是B。例如：

The conjunction “还是” is used in interrogative sentences to indicate a choice. Basic structure: (是) A还是B. For example:

（1）妈妈，是您准备考试还是我准备考试？

（2）你更喜欢打篮球、踢足球还是游泳？

（3）我们什么时候去看电影？今天还是明天？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你吃_____？

B：饺子，我觉得饺子比包子更好吃。

（2）A：你想_____？

B：打车吧，坐公交车没有打车快。

（3）A：你喝_____？

B：奶茶吧，我觉得奶茶比茶和咖啡好喝。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

3 固定格式“要/快/快要/就要……了” Fixed Pattern “要/快/快要/就要……了”

固定格式“要/快/快要/就要……了”表示某事将要发生。如果句子中有时间状语，一般用“就要……了”。例如：

The fixed pattern “要/快/快要/就要……了” is used to indicate that something is about to happen. If the sentence includes a time adverbial, “就要……了” is typically used. For example:

（1）饭菜快要做好了。

（2）火车快开了。

Kùai yào kāixué le. bàba bāng

快要开学了，爸爸帮

dìdi zhǔnbèi shūbāo. běnzi hé bǐ. Wǒ

弟弟准备书包、本子和笔。我

jiù yào kǎoshì le. māma ràng wǒ kàn shū.

就要考试了，妈妈让我看书、

kàn zuòcuò de tí. Wǒmen shàngxué.

看做错的题。我们上学，

bàba. māma bǐ wǒmen hái máng. Wǒ wèn

爸爸、妈妈比我们还忙。我问

tāmen: “Shì wǒ hé dìdi shàngxué hāishi

他们: “是我和弟弟上学还是

nǐmen shàngxué?” Wǒ wènwán. tāmen

你们上学？”我问完，他们

dōu xiào le.

生词 New Word ◇10-8

⑭ 笑 xiào v. smile; laugh

School is about to start, and dad is helping my younger brother prepare his schoolbag, notebooks, and pens. I'm about to take an exam, and mom asked me to read the books and review the questions I got wrong. It feels like our parents are even busier than us when it comes to school. I asked them, "Is it my younger brother and me going to school, or is it you?" After I asked, they both burst out laughing.

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，讨论学习情况，互相询问学会了什么，学得怎么样，有没有做错的题，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. Discuss your learning progress by asking each other about what you have learned, how you are doing, and if you made any mistakes in your work, among other things. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

##### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并描述身体状况和病情。Be able to understand and describe physical conditions and illnesses.

☐ 能听懂并使用动态助词“着”表达动作或状态的持续。Be able to understand and use the aspect particle“着”to express the continuation of an action or a state.

掌握程度副词“最”的用法，能表达某种属性超过所有同类的人或事物。Master the use of the adverb of degree“最”to express that a certain attribute surpasses all similar people or things.

jiāyuè. dōu xiàkè le.

家月，都下课了，

zhe

used after a verb or an adjective to indicate the continuation of an action or a state

Wang Yifei: Jiayue, class is over. Why aren't you going home?

Bai Jiayue: I have a headache. I'm not feeling well.

Wang Yifei: You've been having headaches frequently these days. You should go to the hospital.

Bai Jiayue: I want to rest for a while. I can't move right now—it hurts whenever I move.

Wang Yifei: Then stay here and sit. I'll go get the car and take you to the hospital in a bit.

Bai Jiayue: Thank you, Ms. Wang.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 动态助词“着”（1）Aspect Particle“着”（1）

动态助词“着”用在动词后面，表示动作或状态的持续，否定形式是在动词前面加“没（有）”。例如：

The aspect particle “着” is used after a verb to indicate the continuation of an action or a state. The negative form is created by adding “没（有）” before the verb. For example:

（1）那你在这儿坐着。（2）教室的门开着。

（3）教室的门没开着。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 动态助词“着”（2）Aspect Particle“着”（2）

表示动作或状态持续的句子中，宾语要在动态助词“着”的后面。例如：

In sentences indicating the continuation of an action or a state, the object must be placed after the aspect particle “着”. For example:

（1）现在路上车多，还下着雪，我开慢一点儿。

（2）她穿着白色的裤子。

（3）陈天中没拿着咖啡。

疑问形式有三种：（1）在句尾加“吗”；（2）在句尾加“没有”；（3）动词+没+动词+着。例如：

There are three types of interrogative forms: (1) add “吗” at the end of the sentence; (2) add “没有” at the end of the sentence; and (3) Verb+没+Verb+着. For example:

（1）教室的门开着吗？（2）白家月坐着没有？

（3）她拿没拿着手机？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：安妮，你看见我了吗？我穿着 ___。

B：看见了，你快过来吧。

（2）A：外边 ___ ，你别出去跑步了。

B：好吧，那就在家运动运动。

(3) A: 小明，电视_____?

B：没有，我们都没看电视。

##### Bai Jiayue: Li Wen, please come in!

Li Wen: Jiayue, how are you feeling?

Does your head still hurt?

Bai Jiayue: It doesn't hurt much. The doctor prescribed some medicine, and I feel much better after taking it.

Wang Yifei: Jiayue, do you want something to eat?

Li Wen: That's good to hear!

Li Wen: Eat some. You should eat well when you feel uncomfortable.

Bai Jiayue: What should I eat?

Wang Yifei: You like Chinese food the most, so I'll cook some Chinese dishes. Bai Jiayue: Okay, thank you, Ms. Wang.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 程度副词“最” Adverb of Degree“最”

程度副词“最”用在形容词或心理动词前面，表示某种属性超过所有同类的人或事物。例如：

The adverb of degree “最” is used before adjectives or psychological verbs to indicate that a certain attribute surpasses all similar people or things. For example:

（1）你最喜欢吃中国菜。（2）在我们家，爸爸最高。

（3）你们班谁说中文说得最好？

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（2）A：你最想去哪儿旅游？ B：我 ___。

（3）A：你最喜欢什么颜色？ B：我 ___。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 角色扮演 Role-play

两人一组，一人扮演医生，一人扮演病人。病人头疼，所以来医院看病，医生询问什么时候开始疼的、吃过药没有、最近做过什么，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. One student plays the doctor and the other plays the patient. The patient has a headache and comes to the hospital for treatment. The doctor asks questions such as when the headache started, whether the patient has taken any medicine, what the patient has been doing recently, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

① 事情 shìqíng n.  $ \underline{\text{affair}} $; matter

② 晴 qíng adj. sunny

indicating an

action just

③ 正 zhèng adv. happening or a state just continuing

Wang Yixue: Hello, Jiayue, it's you! Is there anything you need?

Bai Jiayue: Nothing important. I just want to talk to you.

Wang Yixue: Alright. Don't you have class today?

Bai Jiayue: The class is in the afternoon. How's the weather in your place?

Wang Yixue: Although it's sunny in Beijing these days, it's a bit cold.

Bai Jiayue: It's much colder here than in Beijing, and it's snowing outside right now!

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 比较句（4） Comparative Sentences (4)

用 “比” 表示的比较句中，“多了” 或 “得多” 用在形容词后面，表示差别很大。基本结构：A比B+形容词+多了/得多。例如：

In comparative sentences with “比”，“多了” or “得多” is placed after the adjective to indicate a significant difference. Basic structure: A比B+Adjective+多了/得多. For example:

（1）我这里比北京冷多了。

（2）坐飞机比坐火车快得多。

（3）他觉得红茶比绿茶好喝得多。

## 2 比较句（5） Comparative Sentences (5)

用 “比” 表示的比较句中，如果动词带状态补语，“比” 用在动词前后都可以。例如：

In comparative sentences with “比”, if the verb is followed by a complement of state, “比” can be placed either before or after the verb. For example:

（1）昨天比今天下得大。（2）妈妈比我睡得晚。

（3）李文跑得比白家月快。

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 比较句（6） Comparative Sentences (6)

用 “比” 表示的比较句中，如果动词既带宾语，又带状态补语，可以把宾语提前，或者重复动词。如果动词是离合词，需要重复动词性语素。例如：

In comparative sentences with “比”, if the verb takes both an object and a complement of state, the object can be moved to the beginning of the sentence or the verb can be repeated. If the verb is a separable word, the verbal morpheme needs to be repeated. For example:

（1）你跑步跑得比我快。

（2）白家月汉字写得比陈天中好。

（3）他踢足球比我踢得好。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

B：会，但，所以我更喜欢吃妈妈做的菜。

#### 课文4 Text 4

“大晴天”的意思是天气很晴朗。The term “大晴天” means the weather is very sunny.

在房间，白家月在写日记。In the room, Bai Jiayue was writing in her diary.

听两遍课文，判断正误。Listen to the text twice and decide if the statements are true or false. 12-7

（1）今天是个晴天，白家月去外面跑步了。（）

（2）白家月很喜欢跟李文一起跑步。（）

Qian jī tiān tiānqì bù hǎo. wǒ méi zǒulù. měi tiān zuò liǎng zhàn dìtiě qù xuéxiào.

前几天天气不好，我没走路，每天坐两站地铁去学校。

Jīntiān shì gè dà qíng tiān. Lǐ Wén ràng wǒ gēn tā qù wàimiàn pǎobù. Tā xiǎoshīhou jīngcháng今天是个大晴天，李文让我跟他去外面跑步。他小时候经常

pǎobù. pǎo de bǐ wǒ kuài. dànshì tā huì děng wǒ. Gēn Lǐ Wén yìqǐ pǎobù. wǒ hǎo 跑步，跑得比我快，但是他会等我。跟李文一起跑步，我好

gāoxìng a!

高兴啊！

##### 生词 New Words (☐) 12-8

⑨ 站 zhàn n. stop; station

⑩ 小时候 xiǎoshíhou n. childhood

⑪ 好 hǎo adv. very; quite

The weather had been bad over the past few days, so I didn't walk to school; I took the subway for two stops to school every day. Today was a sunny day, and Li Wen asked me to go running outside with him. He often ran when he was a child. He ran faster than me, but he waited for me. Running with Li Wen made me so happy!

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，打电话谈论天气情况，并对比不同地方的天气差异，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. Make a phone call to talk about the weather conditions, compare the weather in different places, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

#### 目标 Objectives

能听懂并使用比较句说明事物之间的具体差别。Be able to understand and use comparative sentences to explain specific differences between things.

能听懂并使用比较句说明事物之间的差别很小。Be able to understand and use comparative sentences to explain the slight differences between things.

掌握双宾语句的用法，能表达给予的意思。Master the use of double-object sentences to express meanings such as giving.

① 新年 xīnnián n. New Year

② 教 jiāo v. teach

③ 花 huā n. flower

④ 希望 xīwàng v. hope; wish Bai Jiayue: Time flies! The New Year is almost here.

Chen Tianzhong: This year, Ms. Wang has been teaching us Chinese, and she works so hard every day.

Bai Jiayue: Yes, she teaches very well. Because of her, we all love attending Chinese class.

Chen Tianzhong: Let's prepare a New Year's gift for her. What do you think we can present to her?

Bai Jiayue: Ms. Wang likes flowers. I think we can give her flowers.

Chen Tianzhong: Then let's go to the flower shop; there are many people buying flowers now. I hope there are still some beautiful flowers in the shop.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 双宾语句（2） Double-Object Sentences (2)

双宾语句是一个动词带两个宾语的句子，一般前一个宾语指人，后一个宾语指事物。本课学习动词“拿、送、卖”加“给”构成的双宾语句。例如：

A double-object sentence is a sentence in which a verb takes two objects. Generally, the first object refers to a person, while the second object refers to a thing. In this lesson, we will learn double-object sentences formed with the verbs “拿”, “送”, and “卖” combined with “给”. For example:

（1）王老师喜欢花，就送给她花吧。

（2）她拿给我一杯水。

（3）他卖给我一本中文书。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：我想送给___，她会喜欢吗？

B：没问题，她的爱好就是读书。

（2）A：昨天过生日，爸爸送 ___。

B：我看看。这个手机很好啊！

（3）A：小雪，你爸爸回来了吗？

B：回来了，他还拿___。

##### 生词 New Words (1) 13-4

⑤ 上面 shàngmiàn n. surface of an object

⑥ 洗手间 xǐshǒujiān n. washroom; restroom

⑦ 里面 lǐmiàn n. inside

⑧ 笔 bǐ m. stroke

Bai Jiayue: Ms. Wang, today's vocabulary has ten more words than yesterday's.

Wang Yifei: Yes! Have you all learned them?

Annie: We've learned them. No problem.

Wang Yifei: Good. Now I'll say them, and you write them down in your notebooks.

Wang Yifei: Class, the character "间" in "洗手间" is wrong. The part inside is "日", not "口".

Bai Jiayue: “日” has one more stroke than “口”. Writing “口” turns it into the “问” in “问题”.

Wang Yifei: Yes, you are right.

分角色朗读对话，读后回答问题。Role-play the dialogue and then answer the questions.

（2）“间”比“问”多几笔？

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 比较句（7） Comparative Sentences (7)

用 “比” 表示的比较句中，数量短语放在形容词后面，表示具体差别。基本结构：A比B+形容词+数量短语。例如：

In comparative sentences using “比”, numeral-measure word phrases are placed after the adjective to indicate the specific difference. Basic structure: A比B+Adjective+Numeral-Measure Word Phrase. For example:

（2）姐姐比我大三岁。

（1）今天的词比昨天多了十个。

（3）坐飞机比坐火车快五个多小时。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：今天你是几点起床的？

B：七点半，比昨天早___。

（2）A：这一课的汉字真多啊！

B：是啊，比第十课多 ___。

（3）A：你比弟弟大几岁？

B：___。

##### 生词 New Words (▶) 13-6

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//017be570-063a-45d8-ab71-9bcce3d7e4ea/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_686_549_718_582.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A32Z%2F-1%2F%2Fc899950bacc5b4130c69309a423902423007fb926e7ce9a73265dd3c88ea9186" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


可能 kěnéng

probably can

⑩ 上网 shàngwǎng v. go online

⑪ 那样 nàyàng pron. so that

Bai Jiayue: It's very pretty. How much is it?

Annie: Jiayue, what do you think of this notebook?

Annie: It's a bit more expensive than the notebook we bought together.

Bai Jiayue: For such a beautiful notebook, it couldn't be just a little more expensive, right?

Annie: I bought it online, and it's really not that expensive. I bought two and will give you one.

Bai Jiayue: Thank you! Then what should I give you in return?

Annie: A coffee cup would be great. I like drinking coffee the most.

Bai Jiayue: Alright! Then we'll both have New Year's gifts!

分角色朗读对话，读后回答问题。Role-play the dialogue and then answer the questions.

（1）白家月觉得这个本子怎么样？

（2）白家月和安妮都有新年礼物了吗？

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//017be570-063a-45d8-ab71-9bcce3d7e4ea/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_94_170_183_257.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A33Z%2F-1%2F%2Febd261763d1f8a30395a6a30497379dda6fc565ff0ece2575bfed02e9b8865dc" alt="Image" width="7%" /></div>


#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 比较句（8） Comparative Sentences (8)

用 “比” 表示的比较句中，“一点儿” 或 “一些” 用在形容词后面，表示差别不大。基本结构：A比B+形容词+一点儿/一些。例如：

In comparative sentences using “比”，“一点儿” or “一些” are placed after the adjective to indicate a small difference. Basic structure: A比B+Adjective+一点儿/一些. For example:

（1）比我们一起买的那个本子贵一点儿。

（2）姐姐比我高一点儿。

（3）那间教室比这间大一些。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你觉得这条白色的裤子长不长？

B： $ \underline{\text{那条黑色的}} $，还是买那条吧。

（2）A：今天的苹果_____昨天的_____。

B：便宜的也很好吃。

（3）A：姐姐的书和本子比我多，所以她的书包 ___。

B：也比你的书包贵一点儿。

#### 课文4 Text 4

在房间，白家月在写日记。In the room, Bai Jiayue was writing in her diary.

听两遍课文，判断正误。Listen to the text twice and decide if the statements are true or false. 13-7

（1）安妮送了白家月一个新年礼物。（）

（2）白家月送了王老师漂亮的花。（）

新年就要到了，安妮送给我一个新本子。她告诉我是在网上买的，比我的本子贵一点儿。我们班同学也送了王老师漂亮的花，希望她高高兴兴地过个新年。

##### 生词 New Words  $ ☐ $ 13-8

⑫ 告诉 gàosu v. tell

⑬ 班 bān n. class

The New Year is almost here. Annie gave me a new notebook. She told me she bought it online, and it's a bit more expensive than mine. Our classmates also gave Ms. Wang beautiful flowers, hoping she will have a happy New Year.

朗读课文，读后选择正确答案。Read the text and then choose the correct answer.

（1）这个新本子贵不贵？（ ）

A 没有白家月的本子贵

B 比白家月的本子贵多了

C 比白家月的本子贵一点儿

（2）同学们为什么送王老师花？（ ）

A 她很漂亮 B 她要回国了 C 新年就要到了

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

新年就要到了，两人一组，互相赠送礼物，并邀请对方来家里聚会，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

The New Year is approaching. Work in pairs and exchange gifts, invite your partner to a party at your home, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//017be570-063a-45d8-ab71-9bcce3d7e4ea/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_193_1332_303_1448.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A34Z%2F-1%2F%2F03cacf14e1e8bcb56a70b92be329da5b0b69d1804c6b4f5243a36d40eaba2195" alt="Image" width="9%" /></div>


A：就要过新年了，我送你一个小礼物。

B：这么好啊，你还给我准备了礼物。

# 一个人过年多没意思啊

# It's boring to celebrate the Spring Festival alone

#### 目标 Objectives

☐ 能听懂并描述某处存在某人或某物。Be able to understand and describe the presence of a person or an object in a certain place.

能听懂并使用复合趋向补语表达动作的方向。Be able to understand and use compound complements of direction to express the direction of an action.

掌握程度副词“多”的用法，能表达程度很高的意思。Master the use of the adverb of degree “多” to express a high degree of something.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 存现句（2） Existential Sentences (2)

动态助词“着”用在动词后面可以构成存现句，动词前面是表示处所的短语，后面一般是不确指的人或事物。基本结构：处所+动词+着+人/事物。例如：

The aspect particle “着” is placed after a verb to form an existential sentence. The phrase before the verb usually indicates a location, and the phrase after the verb generally refers to unspecified people or things. Basic structure: Location+Verb+着+People/Things. For example:

（1）你家楼下站着一个人。

（2）爸爸手里拿着一杯咖啡。

（3）那间教室里坐着不少学生。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：楼下_____，他说他是来找你的。

B：好，我下去看看。

（2）A：本子上面写着什么？ B：_____。

（3）A：天中，你的椅子上_____，你认识他吗？

B：不认识，我们过去问问他。

##### 生词 New Words (1) 14-4

guònian

celebrate the

Spring Festival

没意思 méi yìsi boring

位

wèi

used in deferential reference to people

前面

qiánmiàn n. front

Wang Yifei: Tongle, it really is you! Last time we talked on the phone, you said you'd visit me when you had time. I didn't expect you to come so soon!

Yang Tongle: The Spring Festival is almost here. It's no fun for you to be here all alone, so I come here as soon as possible.

Wang Yifei: I'm so happy you could come!

Yang Tongle: Yifei, who's this next to you?

Wang Yifei: Tongle, this is Li Wen. He's studying medicine at our school. Li Wen, this is my boyfriend, Yang Tongle.

Yang Tongle: Li Wen, nice to meet you!

Li Wen: Nice to meet you too! My home is just in that building up ahead. Feel free to come over when you have time.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 程度副词“多” Adverb of Degree“多”

“多”用在感叹句中，表示程度很高。例如：

“多” is used in exclamatory sentences to express a high degree. For example:

（1）你一个人在这儿多没意思啊！

（2）我们一起去多好啊！

（3）多好看啊！买这件吧。

B：是啊，他们最喜欢在外边玩了。

(3) A: 别买了, 这块手表 ___!

B：没关系，你喜欢就买。

##### 生词 New Words (📐) 14-6

⑦ 房子 fángzi n. house

⑧ 小孩儿 xiǎohái n. child; kid

Yang Tongle: Yifei, your house is really nice, very big, and not far from school.

Wang Yifei: Yes! There's a Chinese family living downstairs, and they are very nice.

Yang Tongle: Then you can ask them for help if you ever need anything.

Wang Yifei: Yes, I also help their child learn Chinese.

Yang Tongle: I remember you told me that child was a girl, and she learned so well.

Wang Yifei: Exactly, she often comes upstairs to play with me.

Yang Tongle: You can ask them when they're free, and I'll treat them to a meal.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 复合趋向补语 Compound Complement of Direction

“上、下、进、出、回、过”加上“来/去”，以及“起来”，用在动词后，可以构成复合趋向补语，表示动作的方向。例如：

Verbs “上”，“下”，“进”，“出”，“回”，and “过”，combined with “来”，“去”，and “起来” are used after a verb to form compound complements of direction, indicating the direction of an action. For example:

（1）她经常跑上来找我玩。（2）楼不高，我们走上去吧。

（3）一听到老师叫他的名字，他就站起来了。

当动词带宾语时，有两种情况：（1）宾语是地点名词，放在“来/去”前面；

##### （2）宾语是事物名词，放在“来/去”前后都可以。例如：

When the verb takes an object, there are two cases: (1) if the object is a location noun, it is placed before “来/去”; (2) if the object is a thing noun, it can be placed either before or after “来/去”. For example:

（1）同学们都走出教室去了。（2）妈妈让我买回一些菜来。

（3）白家月从书包里找出来一个漂亮的本子。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：小雪呢？她不去商场吗？B：她早都___了，在楼下等我们呢。

（2）A：包里是什么东西，快___吧。

B：就是些吃的、喝的，还能有什么？

（3）A：家月病了，我买了点儿药，现在给她 $ \underline{\text{}} $。

B：你等我一下，我跟你一起过去。

Wang Yifei: The exam is about to begin. Please write down your name, and once you're done, you can start answering the questions.

Bai Jiayue: Teacher, I'm finished.

Chen Tianzhong: Teacher, I'm finished too.

Wang Yifei: ... Oh, what are you going to do after the exam?

Bai Jiayue: I want to go to China. I've been there once, but I really want to go again.

Wang Yifei: Good, you can often speak Chinese in China.

分角色朗读对话，读后回答问题。Role-play the dialogue and then answer the questions.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 1 动量补语（1）Complement of Frequency (1)

“数词+次”用在动词后面，构成动量补语，表示动作发生的次数。动词同时带宾语时，如果宾语是人名或地名，放在动量补语前后都可以。例如：

“Numeral+次” is used after a verb to form a complement of frequency, indicating the number of times an action occurs. When the verb is followed by an object, if the object is a person's name or a place name, it can be placed either before or after the complement of frequency. For example:

（2）安妮去过一次北京。

（1）我想去中国，虽然去过一次，但是很想再去一次。

（3）李文见了杨同乐两次。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你去过上海，觉得怎么样？

B：很不错，吃得好，玩得好。

（2）A：安妮来过我们家，她知道怎么走。

B：好，我跟安妮一起过去。

（3）A：你吃过北京烤鸭吗？

B：我吃过_____，很好吃。

Summer Palace

(in Beijing)

Bai Jiayue: The exam is over, so now I can travel abroad.

Li Wen: Where are you planning to go?

Bai Jiayue: I want to go to Beijing again.

Li Wen: Why Beijing again?

Bai Jiayue: Because I want to eat Peking Duck again, drink bubble tea again, and watch a movie at Peking University again...

Li Wen: You want to do a lot of things!

Bai Jiayue: Yes, look, I’ve already bought a ticket for the Summer Palace online.

Li Wen: One of my high school classmates works at the Summer Palace, and he can give you a great tour.

Bai Jiayue: That's amazing! When you travel, the more friends you have, the more paths you have.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 2 动量补语（2）Complement of Frequency (2)

动词既带动量补语，又带宾语时，如果宾语是事物名词，一般放在动量补语后面；如果宾语是代词，放在动量补语前面。例如：

When a verb is followed by both a complement of frequency and an object; if the object is a thing noun, it is generally placed after the complement of frequency; if the object is a pronoun, it is placed before the complement of frequency. For example:

（1）因为我想再吃一次烤鸭，再喝一次奶茶。

（2）我想找他一次。

（3）安妮来过这儿两次。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：你们去北京的时候见到一雪姐姐了吗？

B：我们见了 两次，还去了一次她家。

(2) A: _____?

B：我每个星期给家里打两次电话。

(3) A: _____?

B：就下过一次雪。我觉得今年没有去年冷。

##### 生词 New Words ☑) 15-6

Bai Jiayue: Li Wen, you haven't been back to China for a year, right?

Li Wen: Not quite a year. I went back once this June.

Bai Jiayue: How could I forget? I was the one who took you to the airport.

Li Wen: Yes.

Bai Jiayue: I remember your air ticket was very cheap.

Li Wen: Exactly. Maybe it was because there weren't many people going to Beijing at that time.

Bai Jiayue: The air ticket is a little expensive this time, but I am so happy thinking about going to Beijing.

#### 小语讲堂 Xiaoyu's Classroom

## 3 “有”字句（2）“有”Sentence (2)

“有+数量短语”表示达到一定的数量。例如：

“有+Numeral-Measure Word Phrase” indicates reaching a certain quantity. For example:

（1）你有一年没回国了吧？（2）你女儿今年有10岁了吗？

（3）我有一个月没给家里打电话了。

完成对话。Complete the dialogues.

（1）A：飞北京的机票 8000块钱吗？

B：没有，现在机票便宜。

(2) A: 你男朋友_____?

B：没有，他今年29岁，比我还小一岁呢。

（3）A：我们两个_____？

B：是啊，一年没见，你还是那样。

（1）李文经常回北京，所以不想家。（）

（2）李文、白家月跟鸟一起回北京。（）

我六月的时候回过一次北京，现在有半年多没回去了，我有点儿想家。就要过年了，我要回家过年。家月这次也要去北京，我们都是星期五的飞机。家月说我们好像小鸟，一起飞到北京，再一起飞回这里。

##### 生词 New Words (1) 15-8



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>⑩ 好像</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>hǎoxiàng</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>v.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>be like</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>⑪ 鸟</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>niǎo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>n.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>bird</td></tr></table>

I went back to Beijing once this June, and now it has been over six months since I last went back. I miss home a bit. The Spring Festival is coming, and I'll be going back to celebrate it. Jiayue is also going to Beijing—we're both on the same flight this Friday. Jiayue said we are like little birds, flying to Beijing together and then flying back here together.

（3）儿子就要___上大学了，你给他准备了什么东西啊？

(4) A: 你买的是什么时候的_____?

B: 明天早上的，下午就到北京了。

(5) A: 请您说一下他的_____，我看看有没有这个人。 B: 王大明。

A：不好意思，他没住在我们酒店。

#### 课堂活动 Classroom Activity

##### 双人活动 Pair Work

两人一组，谈论旅游经历，介绍去过哪里、去过几次、吃得怎么样、玩得怎么样以及旅游的感受，等等。尽可能使用本课所学生词和语法。

Work in pairs. Discuss your travel experiences, including where you've been, how many times you've visited, how the food was, how the activities were, and your overall impressions of the trip, and so on. Try to use the vocabulary and grammar learned in this lesson as much as possible.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//6e8bddec-eb58-4380-9c0f-5e33ba70448d/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_200_1415_306_1529.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-08-14T10%3A49%3A25Z%2F-1%2F%2Fa880813a5187387e545ee0dd5e0ac6c20b42f966fa97c2a38f0fc3d9939ef7d5" alt="Image" width="8%" /></div>
